...until it is fixed and persists in the fire. This, however, will not happen on the first attempt; rather, it is necessary to dissolve the fixed substance again, and cook it again until its completion of melting is sufficient for it to penetrate the metal and imbue it. According to the Natural Philosophers <term>Physicos: Early scholars of nature and alchemy who sought to understand the laws of the physical world.</term>, this usually happens during the fourth dissolution and coagulation, the completion of which is the number seven <note>original: "Septenarius." The number seven was highly symbolic in alchemy, representing the seven metals, the seven planets, and the days of creation.</note>.

Therefore, let his philosophical sublimated Mercury <note>original: "sublimatus ☿ Physicus"</note> be taken; let a vessel of this kind be opened, either of glass or a compact material that can resist fire, in which they should be cooked 3 or 4 [times?] <gloss>with continuous fire</gloss> until the Mercury is turned into metal and adheres to the whole vessel. And it is best to repeat this a second time with new Mercury after the first has adhered, until that too adheres. After this has been done, let it be given a fire for 5 or 6 days, somewhat stronger than before; finally, let the whole vessel in which the medicine is located be placed into another lidded iron vessel, and let the fire of reduction be given to the Mercury until everything adhering descends to the bottom of the vessel like a mass of tin or lead.

This mass is the lead, bronze, and Philosophical Tin <term>Stannum Philosophicum: A purified alchemical substance, not common tin, believed to be an essential stage in the creation of the Philosopher's Stone.</term>. When this is produced, it is nothing other (as the Philosophers say) than the blood of women <note>original: "sanguis mulierum," a common alchemical metaphor for a specific mercurial or "menstrual" solvent required for the Work.</note>. Take it, then, and cast away any powdery filth it might have; dissolve it in the remaining Mercury, in 5, 6, 8, or ten parts, grinding it strongly in a <unclear>mortar</unclear> until it can no longer be seen as a separate thing. Place it again in a suitable vessel and cook it as before until all of this is converted into a mass, which you will afterwards reduce just as you did the first time.

When it is taken out, you shall test it to see if it penetrates with sufficient melting <note>original: "fissione," referring to the substance's ability to flow and fuse.</note> upon molten metal or even Saturn <note>The symbol ♄ (Saturn) is the alchemical shorthand for the metal lead.</note>; if it does not remain, [repeat] the dissolution and cooking...

# THE TREASURE OF THE ALCHEMISTS.
## BY THEOPHRASTUS PARACELSUS.

Nature births a mineral in the Bowels of the Earth <note>original: "Visceribus Terræ"</note> of two kinds, which are found in many places in Europe; the best which has come into my hands, experimented upon in Skåne <note>original: "Skoningen," likely referring to the province of Skåne (Scania).</note>, used in a Figure of the Greater World <note>original: "Figuro majoris Mundi," referring to the Macrocosm or the universe as a whole.</note> was the entire Star of the Sphere of the Sun <note>original: "Astrum Sphæræ Solis."</note>. The other is in the Southern Star <note>original: "Astro Meridionali"</note> which always rules the cold; the Glue of the Earth <note>original: "Viscus terre," a sticky, primordial substance</note> is baked through the Star's House in its coagulation, which is found in its whiteness. Therein lie all the causes, and also the ways of minerals, until it has been philosophized that it is cold in the mouth and of a moist nature, and is comparable to the element of water, just like the Stone of true knowledge. And for <term>experience: "experientia," practical knowledge gained through trial and error</term>, all philosophers before me have set down the parts and believed ☿ <note>Mercury</note> and 🜍 <note>Sulfur</note> and 🜔 <note>Salt</note> with the Mother to be in all metals. But they have left us with questions after they took the highest Stones in hand but again 
<margin>than burn themselves</margin>
lay the Mother of all metals, and Spirit forged like water through the <term>Spagyric Art: the alchemical process of separating and then recombining the components of a substance to reach a higher state of purity</term>. Thus it was finally found, which neither <term>Galen: an influential ancient Greek physician</term> nor <term>Avicenna: a Persian polymath and one of the most significant physicians of the Islamic Golden Age</term> brought into their experience; how I myself then, to the eternal physicians, [give] the frame and their joining-together, which <term>Solomon</term> describes for us to <term>coagulate: the process of a liquid changing to a solid or semi-solid state</term>, which was created hard in the first world, fulfilled in the seed of the <unclear>Nolfim</unclear> genus. And there, the beginning and many literal <unclear>arts</unclear> do not belong to the way. I say that in this mineral are found three substances and materials, namely ☿ <note>Mercury</note> & 🜍 <note>Sulfur</note> and a mineral water.

# THE MYSTERIES OF STEPHEN
## OF THE FRATERNITY Φ <note>The Greek letter Phi often represents the "Golden Ratio" or secret alchemical societies associated with the "Golden Circle."</note>

By whatever manner it is established through Nature, and is dissolved out of that into the <term>Spagyric art</term> <note>The alchemical practice of separating a substance into its elemental parts and then recombining them to create a purified, more potent form.</note>, it is through the same spirit which is shared within His spirit, like a form upon a tree here. When the Stone is hidden, but the Holy <term>Stars</term> <note>original: "Astra"; in Paracelsian philosophy, the "Stars" refer to the celestial virtues or "seeds" within matter that give life and form to minerals.</note> appear when Nature comes together, it yields several seed-juices in abundance, secret in their power; subsequently, the power reveals itself with the help of youthful vigor and lives until the ripening of the Stone in its belly. Thus it is also to be understood regarding minerals: that they burn in the <term>Bowels of the Earth</term> <note>original: "Viscere Terre"</note>, whereby the Stars are spread forth. This should be highly regarded by alchemists, who indicate the way to lead the <term>Treasure of Treasures</term> <note>original: "Thesaurum Thesaurorum"</note> through its beginning, middle, and end. In this following treatise, I will describe the Eternal Water's own nature and its own Balsam; through these three, they are dissolved and joined together in many ways through <term>coagulation</term> <note>The process of a liquid thickening or becoming solid.</note>.

<meta>This page continues the practical alchemical instructions from page 243, transitioning from the extraction of the Star of the Earth’s Fire to the distillation of the Spirit and the creation of the Tinctures of Gold and Vitriol.</meta>

Thereupon, it is drawn off by an **alembic** <note>original: "Alembicum"; a classic distillation apparatus consisting of a gourd-like vessel and a cap with a long beak.</note> until no more sharpness of **Aqua Regia** <note>original: "$\nabla$ Regia"; literally "Royal Water," a highly corrosive mixture of acids capable of dissolving gold.</note> can be tasted. You should place this **Spirit** <note>original: "Spiritum"; the volatile, "airy" essence of a substance.</note> into a **retort** <note>original: "Retortorium"; a glass vessel with a long, downward-pointing neck used for distillation.</note> well-sealed with the **Seal of Hermes** <note>original: "Sigillo Hermetis"; an airtight seal, often made by melting the glass neck of the vessel closed.</note>, and set it above to **elevate**—that is, to bring it into its **exaltation** <note>A process intended to increase the power, purity, or "dignity" of a substance.</note>.

And so that a good <unclear>distillation</unclear> <note>original: "diulium"; the script is obscured here, but the context suggests a successful chemical reaction or distillate.</note> occurs, keep the glass thus closed and set it in a **cold place** <note>original: "locum frigidum"</note>; there, it settles itself down finely to be **elevated** and **coagulated** <note>original: "coaguliren"; the process of thickening or solidifying a liquid.</note>. Do this for a third time, and then the **Tincture of Gold** <note>original: "tinctur Solis"; "Sol" or the Sun is the alchemical symbol for Gold.</note> is perfected, and one has certainly paid for the effort in a single work.

Next, take **Copper Vitriol** <note>original: "Venerem Vitriolum"; "Venus" is the alchemical symbol for Copper. Vitriol refers to sulfate minerals.</note> prepared to the highest degree in the **spagyric art** <note>original: "Spagirice"; the alchemical practice of separating and then recombining the constituent parts of a substance (the salt, sulfur, and mercury).</note>. Thereupon, take the "Clement Water" previously reserved; **dissolve** <note>original: "Solvire"</note> it with [water] and proceed further as before with **putrefaction** <note>original: "putrificiren"; the process of breaking down matter through gentle warmth and moisture to allow for new growth.</note> for one month.

Once it has become perfect, you will see the **Sign of the Elements** <note>original: "signum Elementorum"</note> which also separate from one another. In the vessel, there are two kinds of matter: a red substance is found; separate the red from the white. Now, this red **Tincture of Vitriol** is so strong that it clarifies all white **bodies** <note>original: "Corpora"; the solid, material aspects of a substance.</note> into red, or **tinctures** <note>original: "Tingirt"; to permeate a substance with a new color or medicinal virtue.</note> all red bodies into white—which is a wonder.

When this **tincture** is driven through a **retort** <note>original: "per retortam"</note>, you will see a blackness pass over, which is driven through the retort again. Do this as often and as much as necessary until it turns white. Continue the rejuvenation in your <unclear>vial</unclear> <note>original: "Fiukill"; likely a dialect variation of "Phiole" or "vessel."</note>, **perfecting** it until you find the true **green** <note>original: "viridem"</note>, which you will see at the very bottom. ## BOOK OF MERCURIES. PHI.
->From the sayings of Theophrastus Paracelsus regarding Antimony, Gold, and Silver<-
<note>original Latin: "Ex dictis Theophrasti Paracelsi De ♁ ☉ & ☽"</note>

Make a **mercurial wood** <term>☿ holtz</term> <note>likely referring to a branching, crystalline structure known as a "philosophical tree."</note> from 4 pounds of **Gold** [☉], 3 pounds of **Silver** [☽], 4 [parts?] Lead, and 2 **loth** <term>Loth</term> <note>a German unit of weight, roughly 1/32 of a pound.</note> of **amalgam** <term>amalgama</term>. Strike it down <note>precipitate it</note> with a little **Copper**. Thereafter, dissolve with this **Water** 2 loth of a crushed handful of **Antimony** <term>♁</term>. Place it in the fire, and this dissolves 4 loth of Gold [☉]. For the fifth time, pour this water upon it; it runs off very well and does not coagulate until the piece is dissolved. Let the **calx** <term>Kalck</term> <note>the powdery oxide or residue left after heating a metal.</note> strike down again therein from the Antimony [♁] so that the "wood" becomes fine, gentle, and dry. Boil the calx once or [up to] 8 times well with fresh water so that no **sharpness** <note>corrosivity or acidity</note> from the Antimony wood remains with it. Then dry the calx on a gentle fire and weigh the calx of Gold [☉]. If a tenth part of it has evaporated, add to it as much finely crushed **Sulfur** and grind the **Sophic Gold** <term>Sophie ☉</term> to the weight of the whole. Also, calcine it almost entirely; put it into a glass and pour good, strong **distilled vinegar** <term>Acetum distillatum</term> over it.

Crush this stone <note>original: "Selles stein." Possibly referring to a specific mineral or a corruption of "Salis" (salt).</note> and put it into a **pot** <term>Hafen</term>. # Concerning the Preparation of **Copper** <term>♀</term>
<note>original Latin: "De Trio Veneris." "Trio" likely refers to the "strio" or amalgam preparation mentioned above.</note>

Take **Copper** <term>♀</term> beaten very thin and cleanly **purified** <term>purgiret</term> of all impurities. Cut it into small pieces and put them in a **crucible** <term>Tiegl</term> with salt. Thereafter, seal the crucible well on top with a masonry mixture of **loam** <term>Letten</term>. Place it in a brick kiln or furnace for nine days without ceasing. Take ½ lb of red copper and ½ lb of crushed **alum** <term>Alaun</term> <note>a mineral salt often used as a mordant or astringent in historical chemistry</note>; mix them together.

Continue until no more water comes over, then increase the fire. Put this into the bottom... it will foam up at the top. Clean the still-head thoroughly; that is enough. **Note** <note>original: "queris," a corruption of the Latin "quaere" or "query."</note>: place the **distillate** <term>Distillirun.</term> into warm water so that it runs together. Take ½ lb of the **rolled mass** <note>original: "Roll gluck," perhaps referring to a spherical lump or pellet.</note> and that is sufficient. Extract 2 weight units of ½ lb **mercury** <note>symbol: ☿</note>.

## On the Antimony Amalgam <note>original: "De Strio. Antimo." Strio refers to a paste or mixture.</note>

Take one part, finely coarse, yet ensure it is clean of **steel** <term>Stahl</term>. <unclear>Strahlens</unclear> the **gall** <note>original: "cura," possibly referring to the bile or treatment</note> of the **oxen** <term>Ochsen</term>; coat it with that. Take—God grant improvement—well-filtered water and let it stand for 14 days or longer; indeed, the longer it stands, the better it becomes. Afterward, wash the **steel** <term>Stahl</term> so that the **residue** <note>original: "Sudt," referring to the boiled-down sediment</note> comes off but is not driven away. Let the water settle so that a **red slime** sits at the bottom; then gently pour off the water from the top.

# Concerning the Trio of Saturn <note>Symbol: ♄; the alchemical name for lead</note>.

Take soft lead or other lead that contains no silver; if it does, one must cupellate <term>cupeliren</term> <note>a refining process where ores are heated to separate noble metals like gold and silver from base metals like lead</note> the lead as follows; do this one thing: one should add the salt here every hour of the day into the lead, then let it evaporate and afterward saturate it according to how it is formed. But it is best to calcine <term>calcinire</term> <note>heating a substance to high temperatures to reduce it to a powder or oxide</note> the lead as follows: keep the lead molten and poured into a crucible <term>Tiegel</term>, with common salt well mixed into it; rather, put a salt therein like a sand packing <note>original: "Sanndt Dammung"</note>, the salt ground or "Jewish [salt]" <note>original: "Judisch"; likely referring to *Sal Judai* or Bitumen of Judea, used in various alchemical processes</note> placed beneath it so that the salt remains entirely with the lead. The salt should be completely dried, yet stirred constantly, and when it has become dry, proceed thus:

Take 10 **loth** <term>loth</term> <note>a unit of weight, approximately 15–18 grams</note> of the lead; one measure of vinegar; [take] calcined lead <note>Symbol: ♄</note>; and give the gold <note>Symbol: ☉</note> into the paste; put the lead <note>Symbol: ♄</note> into the fire <note>Symbol: 🜂</note> so that it goes over it; let it lie therein for 36 hours. When it cools from the fire, then stir it; below, have a smooth marble slab, freshly laid—the larger it is, the better—then place it in a damp place with a wooden stick beneath it, so that whatever runs off flows into the basin. Atop the stone, one makes an iron lid according to the size of the stone, which on the other side is one thumb high <note>approximately one inch</note> and sloped down like a gutter <note>original: "Phirinn"; likely meaning a channel or spout</note>, so that the gold <note>Symbol: ☉</note> could flow down. It is depicted herewith, showing the white colors; a further measure together with the beautiful matter, and add to it the following water or oil:

Take <note>Symbol: ℞</note> 2 pounds [of liquid], 2 **loth** of antimony <note>Symbol: ♁</note>, 5 **loth** of salt <note>Symbol: ⊕</note>, 1 **loth** of tragacanth <term>tragant</term> <note>a natural gum used as a binder</note>, and well-known saltpeter; 4 **loth** of aqua fortis <term>macerire fortis</term> <note>Nitric acid, a powerful solvent</note>, all pounded small; mix with old urine, moistened, then distilled; so it produces a water of yellow color.

# On the Regimen of Mercury

The composition of the sulfur, which is called Mercury <note>original: ☿; in alchemical theory, sulfur and mercury are the two "parents" of all metals</note>, with Mercury <unclear>v:</unclear> as well as with a little Ferment <term>Ferment; a substance added to a base metal to "leaven" it and trigger its transformation into gold or silver</term>, dissolves many kinds of Gold <note>original: ☉</note> and Silver <note>original: ☽</note> into a liquid. Through Art, this resolves them into a living state <note>original Latin: *vivum*</note> from the bodies, when the young [metals] are amalgamated with the Mercury.

## Ferment

This Stone <term>Stone; the Philosopher's Stone, the legendary substance capable of transmuting base metals and granting long life</term> is a strongly penetrating spirit. Its father is the Sun <note>original: ☉</note>; its mother is the Moon <note>original: ☽</note>! <note>This is a direct reference to the "Emerald Tablet," a foundational hermetic text</note> Believe, son of both, that our entire work depends upon the regulation of the fire and the grasp of the intellect. For whoever seeks not only the stone but possesses the power of the fire [will succeed], because it requires neither great strength nor great labor; for it is almost brought to pass by a single material, so that in its water it then coagulates itself, sublimates itself, distills itself, and through the exchanged water of its blood, it cleanses itself—all within one vessel, without the help of any thing made by hands. For the entire mastery in this art is nothing other than, first, a right solution <term>Solution; the process of turning a solid into a liquid</term> and a perfect and natural coagulation <term>Coagulation; the process of thickening or solidifying a liquid back into a solid</term>. Plato says: fire [acts] and it coagulates as soon as the young vapor [appears].

# On the Mercury of the Philosophers

It is called our Salt or the Salt of the Metals, the Mercury of the Philosophers. And the Mercury of the Philosophers is nothing other than a body dissolved into a pure water.

# Rubric concerning <unclear>the recent ones</unclear>

Dissolve the amalgam by means of wine, then separate it; and if there is anything remaining in the bottom, place it over a strong fire in a crucible, and there is produced a certain red color like Blood. Pour fresh water over it and draw it off again; do this three times. And in such a <term>Rubification</term> <note>original: *Rubificatione*; the process of reddening, the final stage of the alchemical Great Work</note> add to it the <term>Soul</term> <note>original: *animam*; the volatile essence or spirit of the substance</note> and the <term>Sun</term> <note>original: *solem*; the alchemical symbol and name for gold</note> and <term>Moons</term> <note>original: *lunas*; the alchemical symbol and name for silver</note>. Prepare this reddened gold and dissolve it under <term>dung</term> <note>original: *fimo*; horse dung was used in laboratories as a source of steady, gentle, and prolonged heat during fermentation or digestion</note> for 40 days, and it becomes a red water. Over 80 ounces of this solution, place 3 drams of the aforementioned medicine and place it in a cold spot, and there will be little red stones. Dissolve these under dung and coagulate them again; dissolve its water, and one part by weight will convert 1,000 parts, and it is prepared into the truest <term>Sun</term> <note>gold</note>.

### There follows the method of making the dissolving 
### water of gold.

Take natural Gold and by means of it, with mercury itself, make mercurial water, always returning Mercury to Mercury. And thus you will have the <term>Water of Life of the Philosophers</term> <note>original: *aqua Vite Philosophorum*</note> as it is called; and it is <term>potable gold</term> <note>original: *aurum potabile*; a legendary liquid medicine made from gold, believed to grant longevity</note> which has the power to cure all ailments of any body, from <unclear>whatever</unclear> cause they were brought about. And it is not the "water of life" of fools made from wine <note>The author is distinguishing the alchemical *Aqua Vitae* from ordinary distilled alcohol (brandy), which was also called aqua vitae</note>. But it is the Water of Life of us, or of the Wise, which it is not permitted to reveal.

## The Dissolution of Bodies is twofold.

Namely, first through fire into mercury; second, into mercurial water.

<summary>This page discusses the mystical correlation between the alphabet and numerical systems (9, 5, 10, and 100), suggesting that these mathematical "roots" reveal eternal truths hidden within language. It references the use of "hieroglyphic" symbols and the role of Mercury as a mediating force in understanding these sacred names.</summary>
